## Narration 1945

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A2022)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 7</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ بَشَّارٍ، حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدٌ، - وَهُوَ ابْنُ جَعْفَرٍ - حَدَّثَنَا شُعْبَةُ، عَنْ عَمْرٍو، قَالَ سَمِعْتُ جَابِرَ بْنَ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم خَطَبَ فَقَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ إِذَا جَاءَ أَحَدُكُمْ يَوْمَ الْجُمُعَةِ وَقَدْ خَرَجَ الإِمَامُ فَلْيُصَلِّ رَكْعَتَيْنِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 2022]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2022" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 2022</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   2022 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Jabir b. &#x27;Abdullah said that the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) delivered the sermon and said: When any one of you comes for the Friday (prayer) and the Imam comes out (from his apartment), (even then) should observe two rak&#x27;ahs (of prayer)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 2022]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2022" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">शुअबा ने अम्र (बिन दीनार) से रिवायत की, उन्हों ने कहा: मैं ने हज़रत जाबिर बिन अब्दुल्लाह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से सुना के नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने ख़ुत्बे में फ़रमाया: जब तुम में से कोई शख़्स जुम्मे के दिन आए जबके इमाम (घर से) निकल (कर) आ चुका है तो वह दो रकात पढ़ ले।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 2022]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2022" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">شعبہ نے عمرو ( بن دینار ) سے روایت کی ، انھوں نے کہا : میں نے حضرت جابر بن عبداللہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے سنا کہ نبی اکرم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے خطبے میں فرمایا : &quot; جب تم میں سے کوئی شخص جمعے کے دن آئے جبکہ امام ( گھر سے ) نکل ( کر ) آچکا ہے تو وہ دو رکعت پڑھ لے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 2022]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2022" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Shuba ne Amr (bin Dinar) se riwayat ki, unhone kaha: Maine Hazrat Jabir bin Abdullah (Radi Allahu Anhu) se suna ke Nabi Akram (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne khutbay mein farmaya: Jab tum mein se koi shakhs jummay ke din aaye jabke Imam (ghar se) nikal (kar) aa chuka hai toh woh do rakat padh le.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 2022]</p>

## Narration 1946

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A2023)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 7</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنَا قُتَيْبَةُ بْنُ سَعِيدٍ، حَدَّثَنَا لَيْثٌ، ح وَحَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ رُمْحٍ، أَخْبَرَنَا اللَّيْثُ، عَنْ أَبِي الزُّبَيْرِ، عَنْ جَابِرٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ جَاءَ سُلَيْكٌ الْغَطَفَانِيُّ يَوْمَ الْجُمُعَةِ وَرَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَاعِدٌ عَلَى الْمِنْبَرِ فَقَعَدَ سُلَيْكٌ قَبْلَ أَنْ يُصَلِّيَ فَقَالَ لَهُ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ أَرَكَعْتَ رَكْعَتَيْنِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ لاَ ‏.‏ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ قُمْ فَارْكَعْهُمَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 2023]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2023" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 2023</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   2023 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Jabir reported that Sulaik Ghatafani came on Friday (for prayer) while the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) was sitting on the pulpit. Sulaik also sat down before observing prayer. The Apostle of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said: Have you observed two rak&#x27;ahs? He said: No. He (the Holy Prophet) said: Stand and observe them</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 2023]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2023" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 2023</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   2023 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू जुबैर ने हज़रत जाबिर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत की के सुलैक गहतफनी (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) जुम्मे के दिन आये जबके रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) मिम्बर पर बैठे हुए थे तोह सुलैक नमाज़ पढ़ने से पहले ही बैठ गए. रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने उनसे कहा: क्या तुमने दो रकअतें पढ़ ली हैं? उन्होंने कहा: नहीं. आपने फ़रमाया: उठो और दो रकअतें पढ़ो.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 2023]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2023" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابو زبیر نے حضرت جابر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت کی کہ سلیک غطفانی رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ جمعے کے دن آئے جبکہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم منبر پر بیٹھے ہوئے تھے تو سلیک نماز پڑھنے سے پہلے ہی بیٹھ گئے ۔ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے ان سے کہا : &quot; کیا تم نے دو رکعتیں پڑھ لی ہیں؟ &quot; انھوں نے کہا : نہیں ۔ آپ نے فرمایا : &quot; اٹھو اور دو ر کعتیں پڑھو ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 2023]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2023" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Zubair ne Hazrat Jabir (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat ki ke Sulaik Ghatafani (Radi Allahu Anhu) jummay ke din aaye jabke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) mimbar par baithe hue the toh Sulaik namaz padhne se pehle hi baith gaye. Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne unse kaha: Kya tumne do rakatein padh li hain? Unhone kaha: Nahi. Aapne farmaya: Utho aur do rakatein padho.</p>

## Narration 1947

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A2024)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 7</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The Messenger of Allah addressing Sulaik and then giving the general two-rakah instruction. All five fields supply Sulaiks arrival/sitting, the personal short-prayer command and the following general instruction to arrivals during the Friday sermon. Preserve the full finite body and the original source spelling defects.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنَا إِسْحَاقُ بْنُ إِبْرَاهِيمَ، وَعَلِيُّ بْنُ خَشْرَمٍ، كِلاَهُمَا عَنْ عِيسَى بْنِ يُونُسَ، - قَالَ ابْنُ خَشْرَمٍ أَخْبَرَنَا عِيسَى، - عَنِ الأَعْمَشِ، عَنْ أَبِي سُفْيَانَ، عَنْ جَابِرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، قَالَ جَاءَ سُلَيْكٌ الْغَطَفَانِيُّ يَوْمَ الْجُمُعَةِ وَرَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَخْطُبُ فَجَلَسَ فَقَالَ لَهُ ‏ &quot;‏ يَا سُلَيْكُ قُمْ فَارْكَعْ رَكْعَتَيْنِ وَتَجَوَّزْ فِيهِمَا - ثُمَّ قَالَ - إِذَا جَاءَ أَحَدُكُمْ يَوْمَ الْجُمُعَةِ وَالإِمَامُ يَخْطُبُ فَلْيَرْكَعْ رَكْعَتَيْنِ وَلْيَتَجَوَّزْ فِيهِمَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 2024]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2024" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The Messenger of Allah addressing Sulaik and then giving the general two-rakah instruction. All five fields supply Sulaiks arrival/sitting, the personal short-prayer command and the following general instruction to arrivals during the Friday sermon. Preserve the full finite body and the original source spelling defects.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Jabir b. &#x27;Abdullah reported: Sulaik Ghatafani came on Friday when the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) was delivering the sermon. He (Sulaik) sat down. He (the Holy Prophet) said to him: 0 Sulaik I stand and observe two rak&#x27;ahs and make them short, and then said: When any one of you comes on Friday, while the Imam delivers the sermon, he should observe two rak&#x27;ahs and should make them short</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 2024]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2024" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The Messenger of Allah addressing Sulaik and then giving the general two-rakah instruction. All five fields supply Sulaiks arrival/sitting, the personal short-prayer command and the following general instruction to arrivals during the Friday sermon. Preserve the full finite body and the original source spelling defects.</p>

<p dir="auto">अबु सुफियान (तल्हा बिन नफ़ी वास्ति) ने हज़रत जाबिर बिन अब्दुल्लाह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत की गहतफनी (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) जुम्मे के दिन (उस वक़्त) आये जब रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) खुत्बा दे रहे थे तोह वह बैठ गए. आपने उनसे कहा: ए सुलैक! उठ कर दो रकअतें पढ़ो और इन में इख्तिसार बरतो. इसके बाद आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: जब तुम में से कोई जुम्मे के दिन आये जबकि इमाम खुत्बा दे रहा हो तोह वह दो रकअतें पढ़े और इन में इख्तिसार करे.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 2024]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2024" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The Messenger of Allah addressing Sulaik and then giving the general two-rakah instruction. All five fields supply Sulaiks arrival/sitting, the personal short-prayer command and the following general instruction to arrivals during the Friday sermon. Preserve the full finite body and the original source spelling defects.</p>

<p dir="rtl">ابو سفیان ( طلحہ بن نافع واسطی ) نے حضرت جابر بن عبداللہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت کی ، انھوں نےکہا : سلیک غطفانی رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ جمعے کے دن ( اس وقت ) آئے جب رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم خطبہ دے رہے تھے تو وہ بیٹھ گئے ۔ آپ نے ان سے کہا : &quot; اے سلیک!ا ٹھ کر دو رکعتیں پڑھو اور ان میں سے اختصار برتو ۔ &quot; اس کے بعد آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا! &quot; جب تم میں سے کوئی جمعے کےدن آئے جبکہ امام خطبہ دے رہا ہو تو وہ دو رکعتیں پڑھے اور ان میں اختصار کرے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 2024]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2024" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The Messenger of Allah addressing Sulaik and then giving the general two-rakah instruction. All five fields supply Sulaiks arrival/sitting, the personal short-prayer command and the following general instruction to arrivals during the Friday sermon. Preserve the full finite body and the original source spelling defects.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Sufiyan (Talha bin Nafi Wasti) ne Hazrat Jabir bin Abdullah (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat ki, unhone kaha: Sulaik Ghatafani (Radi Allahu Anhu) jummay ke din (uss waqt) aaye jab Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) khutba de rahe the toh woh baith gaye. Aapne unse kaha: Aye Sulaik! Uth kar do rakatein padho aur in mein ikhtisar barto. Iske baad aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Jab tum mein se koi jummay ke din aaye jabke Imam khutba de raha ho toh woh do rakatein padhe aur in mein ikhtisar kare.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 2024]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2024" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1948

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A2025)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 7</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Abu Rifaa describing the Messenger leaving his sermon to teach him and then completing the sermon. Each complete field supplies the strangers request, the Messengers approach and teaching, the chair with legs the narrator thought were iron, and the return to finish the sermon. Preserve the narrators uncertainty rather than turning the chair material into a certainty.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنَا شَيْبَانُ بْنُ فَرُّوخَ، حَدَّثَنَا سُلَيْمَانُ بْنُ الْمُغِيرَةِ، حَدَّثَنَا حُمَيْدُ بْنُ هِلاَلٍ، قَالَ قَالَ أَبُو رِفَاعَةَ انْتَهَيْتُ إِلَى النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَهُوَ يَخْطُبُ قَالَ فَقُلْتُ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ رَجُلٌ غَرِيبٌ جَاءَ يَسْأَلُ عَنْ دِينِهِ لاَ يَدْرِي مَا دِينُهُ - قَالَ - فَأَقْبَلَ عَلَىَّ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَتَرَكَ خُطْبَتَهُ حَتَّى انْتَهَى إِلَىَّ فَأُتِيَ بِكُرْسِيٍّ حَسِبْتُ قَوَائِمَهُ حَدِيدًا - قَالَ - فَقَعَدَ عَلَيْهِ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَجَعَلَ يُعَلِّمُنِي مِمَّا عَلَّمَهُ اللَّهُ ثُمَّ أَتَى خُطْبَتَهُ فَأَتَمَّ آخِرَهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 2025]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2025" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Abu Rifaa describing the Messenger leaving his sermon to teach him and then completing the sermon. Each complete field supplies the strangers request, the Messengers approach and teaching, the chair with legs the narrator thought were iron, and the return to finish the sermon. Preserve the narrators uncertainty rather than turning the chair material into a certainty.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Rifa&#x27;a reported: I came to the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) when he was delivering the sermon, and I said: Messenger of Allah, here is a stranger and he wants to learn about this religion and he does not know what this religion is. The Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) looked at me and left his sermon till he came to me, and he was given a chair and I thought that Its legs were made of iron. The Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) sat In it and he began to teach me what Allah had taught him. He then came (to the pulpit) for his sermon and completed it to the end</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 2025]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2025" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Abu Rifaa describing the Messenger leaving his sermon to teach him and then completing the sermon. Each complete field supplies the strangers request, the Messengers approach and teaching, the chair with legs the narrator thought were iron, and the return to finish the sermon. Preserve the narrators uncertainty rather than turning the chair material into a certainty.</p>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत अबू रिफ़ाअ (तमीम बिन उसय्यद अदवी (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु)) ने कहा के मैं नबी (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास (उस वक़्त) पहुँचा जबके आप ख़ुत्बा दे रहे थे, मैं ने अर्ज़ की: ऐ अल्लाह के रसूल (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम)! एक परदेसी आदमी है अपने दीन के बारे में पूछने आया है उसे मालूम नहीं के उसका दीन क्या है। कहा: तो रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) मेरी तरफ़ मुतवज्जह हुए और अपना ख़ुत्बा छोड़ा, यहाँ तक के मेरे पास पहुँच गए। एक कुर्सी लाई गई मेरे ख़्याल में उस के पाये लोहे के थे, कहा: तो रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) उस पर बैठ गए और अल्लाह तआला ने जो कुछ आपको सिखाया था उस में से मुझे सिखाने लगे, फिर अपने ख़ुत्बे के लिए बढ़े और उसका आख़िरी हिस्सा मुकम्मल फ़रमाया।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 2025]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2025" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Abu Rifaa describing the Messenger leaving his sermon to teach him and then completing the sermon. Each complete field supplies the strangers request, the Messengers approach and teaching, the chair with legs the narrator thought were iron, and the return to finish the sermon. Preserve the narrators uncertainty rather than turning the chair material into a certainty.</p>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت ابو رفاعہ ( تمیم بن اُسید عدوی رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ ) نے کہا کہ میں نبی صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے پاس ( اس وقت ) پہنچا جبکہ آپ خطبہ دے رہے تھے ، میں نے عرض کی : اے اللہ کے رسول صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم !ایک پردیسی آدمی ہے اپنے دین کے بارے میں پوچھنے آیا ہے اسے معلوم نہیں کہ ا س کادین کیا ہے کہا : تو رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم میری طرف متوجہ ہوئے اور اپنا خطبہ چھوڑا ، یہاں تک کہ میرے پاس پہنچ گئے ۔ ایک کرسی لائی گئی میرے خیال میں اس کے پائے لوہے کے تھے ، کہا : تو رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم اس پر بیٹھ گئے اور اللہ تعالیٰ نے جو کچھ آپ کو سکھایا تھا اس میں سے مجھے سکھانے لگے پھر اپنے خطبے کے لئے بڑھے اور اس کا آخری حصہ مکمل فرمایا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 2025]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2025" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Abu Rifaa describing the Messenger leaving his sermon to teach him and then completing the sermon. Each complete field supplies the strangers request, the Messengers approach and teaching, the chair with legs the narrator thought were iron, and the return to finish the sermon. Preserve the narrators uncertainty rather than turning the chair material into a certainty.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Abu Rifa&#x27;a (Tamim bin Usayd Adawi (Radi Allahu Anhu)) ne kaha ke main Nabi (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas (uss waqt) pahuncha jabke aap khutba de rahe the, maine arz ki: Aye Allah ke Rasool (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam)! Ek pardesi aadmi hai apne deen ke baare mein puchne aaya hai usey maloom nahi ke uska deen kya hai. Kaha: Toh Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) meri taraf mutawajjah hue aur apna khutba chhoda, yahan tak ke mere paas pahunch gaye. Ek kursi layi gayi mere khayal mein uske paye lohay ke the, kaha: Toh Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) us par baith gaye aur Allah Ta&#x27;ala ne jo kuch aapko sikhaya tha usmein se mujhe sikhane lage, phir apne khutbay ke liye badhe aur uska aakhri hissa mukammal farmaya.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 2025]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2025" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1949

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A2026)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 7</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ مَسْلَمَةَ بْنِ قَعْنَبٍ، حَدَّثَنَا سُلَيْمَانُ، - وَهُوَ ابْنُ بِلاَلٍ - عَنْ جَعْفَرٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنِ ابْنِ أَبِي رَافِعٍ، قَالَ اسْتَخْلَفَ مَرْوَانُ أَبَا هُرَيْرَةَ عَلَى الْمَدِينَةِ وَخَرَجَ إِلَى مَكَّةَ فَصَلَّى لَنَا أَبُو هُرَيْرَةَ الْجُمُعَةَ فَقَرَأَ بَعْدَ سُورَةِ الْجُمُعَةِ فِي الرَّكْعَةِ الآخِرَةِ ‏{‏ إِذَا جَاءَكَ الْمُنَافِقُونَ‏}‏ - قَالَ - فَأَدْرَكْتُ أَبَا هُرَيْرَةَ حِينَ انْصَرَفَ فَقُلْتُ لَهُ إِنَّكَ قَرَأْتَ بِسُورَتَيْنِ كَانَ عَلِيُّ بْنُ أَبِي طَالِبٍ يَقْرَأُ بِهِمَا بِالْكُوفَةِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ أَبُو هُرَيْرَةَ إِنِّي سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَقْرَأُ بِهِمَا يَوْمَ الْجُمُعَةِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 2026]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2026" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn Abu Rafi&#x27; said: Marwan appointed Abu Huraira as his deputy in Medina and he himself left for Mecca. Abu Huraira led us in the Jumu&#x27;a prayer and recited after Surah Jumu&#x27;a in the second rak&#x27;ah: &quot; When the hypocrites came to thee&quot; (Surah 63). I then met Abu Huraira as he came back and said to him: You have recited two surahs which &#x27;Ali bin Abu Talib used to recite in Kufah. Upon this Abu Huraira said: I heard the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) reciting these two in the Friday (prayer)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 2026]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2026" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सुलैमान जो हिलाल (तमीमी) के बेटे हैं अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के बेटे उबैदुल्लाह) से रिवायत की अल्लाहु अन्हु) को मदीना में अपना जानशीन मुकर्रर किया और खुद मक्काः चला गया. हज़रात अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने हमें जुम्मे की नमाज़ पढ़ाई और (पहली रकात में) सौराह जुमु&#x27;यह पढ़ने के बाद दूसरी रकात में &#x27;इज़ा जा&#x27;अक अल-मुनाफ़िक़ून&#x27; पढ़ी और कहा: जब अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) जुम्मे की नमाज़ से फारिग हुए तोह मैं उनके पास जा पहुंचा और उनसे कहा: आपने वह दो सूरतें पढ़ी हैं जो अली इब्न तालीब (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कूफ़ा में पढ़ा करते थे. अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: मैंने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को जुम्मे के दिन ये सूरतें पढ़ते हुए सुना है.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 2026]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2026" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">سلیمان جو ہلال ( تمیمی ) کے بیٹے ہیں ، انھوں نے جعفر ( صادق ) سے ، انھوں نے اپنے والد سے اور انھوں نے ابو رافع ( مولیٰ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے بیٹے عبیداللہ ) سے ر وایت کی ، انھوں نے کہا : مروان نے حضرت ابو ہریرۃ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ کو مدینہ میں اپنا جانشین مقرر کیا اور خود مکہ چلا گیا ۔ حضرت ابو ہریرۃ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے ہمیں جمعے کی نماز پڑھائی اور ( پہلی رکعت میں ) سورہ جمعہ پڑھنے کے بعد دوسری رکعت میں إِذَا جَاءَكَ الْمُنَافِقُونَ پڑھی اور کہا : جب ابو ہریرۃ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ جمعے کی نماز سے فارغ ہوئے تو میں ان کے پاس جاپہنچا اور ان سے کہا : آپ نے وہ دو سورتیں پڑھی ہیں جو علی ابن طالب رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ کوفہ میں پڑھا کرتے تھے ۔ ابو ہریرۃ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے کہا : میں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کو جمعے کے دن یہ سورتیں پڑھتے ہوئے سنا ہے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 2026]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2026" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Sulaiman jo Hilal (Tamimi) ke bete hain, unhone Jafar (Sadiq) se, unhone apne walid se aur unhone Abu Rafe (Maula Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke bete Ubaidullah) se riwayat ki, unhone kaha: Marwan ne Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) ko Madina mein apna janasheen muqarrar kiya aur khud Makkah chala gaya. Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne hamein jummay ki namaz padhayi aur (pehli rakat mein) Surah Jumu&#x27;ah padhne ke baad dusri rakat mein &#x27;Iza ja&#x27;aka al-munafiqun&#x27; padhi aur kaha: Jab Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) jummay ki namaz se farigh hue toh main unke paas ja pahuncha aur unse kaha: Aapne woh do suratein padhi hain jo Ali ibn Talib (Radi Allahu Anhu) Kufa mein padha karte the. Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Maine Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko jummay ke din ye suratein padhte hue suna hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 2026]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2026" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">This reading preserves separate source spans. Intervening source notes are retained under Comments. Report type: companion report. Attribution: Ubayd Allah ibn Abi Rafi reporting Marwans appointment of Abu Huraira and the supplied recitation detail. The four non-English fields supply the appointment of Abu Huraira and a finite two-rakah recitation variant. Retain those supplied portions and exclude the comparison/pointer wrappers. English supplies only the recitation variant and leaves its reciter unnamed; it receives its own qualified abbreviated-transmission attribution. The surah incipits identify the supplied recitation and are not separate hadiths. Some reports contain separate source passages. Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنَا قُتَيْبَةُ بْنُ سَعِيدٍ، وَأَبُو بَكْرِ بْنُ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ قَالاَ حَدَّثَنَا حَاتِمُ بْنُ إِسْمَاعِيلَ، ح وَحَدَّثَنَا قُتَيْبَةُ، حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ الْعَزِيزِ، - يَعْنِي الدَّرَاوَرْدِيَّ - كِلاَهُمَا عَنْ جَعْفَرٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي رَافِعٍ، قَالَ اسْتَخْلَفَ مَرْوَانُ أَبَا هُرَيْرَةَ ‏.‏ </p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p dir="rtl">فَقَرَأَ بِسُورَةِ الْجُمُعَةِ فِي السَّجْدَةِ الأُولَى وَفِي الآخِرَةِ ‏{‏ إِذَا جَاءَكَ الْمُنَافِقُونَ‏}‏ </p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 2027]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2027" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<p dir="auto">This reading preserves separate source spans. Intervening source notes are retained under Comments. Report type: companion report. The surah incipits identify the supplied recitation and are not separate hadiths. Some reports contain separate source passages. Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="auto">हातिम बिन इस्माइल और अब्दुल अज़ीज़ दरावर्दी दोनों ने (अपनी अपनी सनद के साथ) जाफ़र से रिवायत की, उन्हों ने अपने वालिद से और उन्हों ने उबैदुल्लाह बिन अबी राफ़े से रिवायत की, उन्हों ने कहा: मरवान (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने हज़रत अबू हुरैरा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) को क़ायम मुक़ाम गवर्नर बनाया</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p dir="auto">उन्हों ने पहली रकात में सूरह जुमुआ पढ़ी और दूसरी रकात में &#x27;इज़ा जाअका अल-मुनाफ़िक़ून&#x27;।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 2027]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2027" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="rtl">حاتم بن اسماعیل اور عبدالعزیز دراوردی دونوں نے ( اپنی اپنی سندکے ساتھ ) جعفر سے روایت کی ، انھوں نے اپنے والد سے اور انھوں نے عبیداللہ بن ابی رافع سے روایت کی ، انھوں نے کہا : مروان رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے حضرت ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ کو قائم مقام گورنر بنایا</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p dir="rtl">انھوں نے پہلی رکعت میں سورہ جمعہ پڑھی اور دوسری رکعت میں إِذَا جَاءَكَ الْمُنَافِقُونَ ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 2027]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/2027" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="ltr">Hatim bin Ismail aur Abdul Aziz Darawardi dono ne (apni apni sanad ke sath) Jafar se riwayat ki, unhone apne walid se aur unhone Ubaidullah bin Abi Rafe se riwayat ki, unhone kaha: Marwan (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) ko qaim muqam governor banaya</p>
